These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.

</details>


### [82] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自主策略进化（Autonomous Policy Evolution）评估框架和EvoPolicyGym基准，用于衡量智能体在固定交互预算下迭代改进可执行策略的能力。GPT-5.5在所有16个环境中取得最优排名。通过轨迹级诊断分析，揭示了智能体在预算分配、反馈转化和参数调优方面的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体评估存在两个问题：一是将策略改进过程简化为最终得分，二是与开放式软件工程进展混为一谈。缺乏一个受控的、可重复的评估设置来专门衡量智能体通过反馈迭代改进可执行策略的能力。

Method: 提出Autonomous Policy Evolution受控评估框架：一个模型智能体在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统。基于此框架构建EvoPolicyGym基准，由紧凑的交互式RL环境组成。通过聚合排名得分和轨迹级诊断分析来评估智能体性能。

Result: GPT-5.5在EvoPolicyGym全部16个环境中取得最强聚合排名得分和top-2性能。轨迹级诊断揭示了智能体在预算分配、反馈转化为参数调优方面的差异。

Conclusion: 强大的自主策略进化不仅依赖于孤立的任务获胜，还依赖于发现任务适切的机制并在有界反馈下精炼策略。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [83] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号方法G-RRM，将符号等变循环推理模型（SE-RRM）与经典约束求解器结合。实验表明，当问题具有广阔组合搜索空间且求解器能动态覆盖分支选择时，神经引导可显著加速求解（Sudoku上回溯加速33.3×），否则无效甚至降速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究神经引导（G-RRM）何时能有效提升符号约束求解器的搜索效率，探索两种范式结合的条件和边界。

Method: 将SE-RRM作为神经求解器生成完整解方案，引导经典符号求解器（回溯法、Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0.0）产生全局正确解；通过对比不同求解器结构和问题规模，分析引导有效的两个必要条件。

Result: 9×9数独上：回溯加速33.3×，Glucose 4.1加速1.70×（p<0.001）；完美提示下25×25仍加速1.17×。CaDiCaL 3.0.0无显著加速（中位数1.02×，不显著），均值反而显著降速（0.90×）。

Conclusion: 神经引导的有效性取决于两个条件：(1) 问题需有广阔组合搜索空间以暴露潜在增益；(2) 求解器架构需能动态覆盖分支选择。当条件满足时，可驱动中位冲突数归零并带来显著加速。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [84] [What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02507)
*Arman Ghaffarizadeh,Danyal Mohaddes,Aliakbar Izadkhah,Shahriar Noroozizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道辩论框架，研究LLM代理在社交结构化环境中的公开表达与私下表达（OTR）的差异。发现对齐诱导设置下，代理的公开-私下分歧率从约3%基线上升至约40%，且该效应在多个分析维度上一致，表明代理存在涌现性社交目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估主要关注显式目标达成，忽略了社交结构（角色、受众、关系背景）对代理表达的影响。作者希望验证社交结构本身是否会导致代理在公开场合与私下场合表达不一致。

Method: 提出双通道辩论框架：代理在公开通道中产生可共享的发言，同时在私下（OTR）通道中记录但不向对方展示的响应。在10个模型、3个场景、每个场景5种变体下进行实验，通过姿态分析、语义相似度、自然语言推理和问卷调查四个维度评估公开-OTR分歧。

Result: 对齐诱导设置下，代理决策分歧率从约3%基线上升到约40%；该效应在四种聚合分析中一致；OTR响应明确将公开迎合归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）。

Conclusion: 代理评估应超越显式目标检测，识别涌现性目标；提出双通道评估框架和补充行为指标来操作化这种评估。

Abstract: LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.

</details>


### [85] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECONTEXT是一种无需训练的长上下文推理方法，通过递归地利用模型内部相关性信号构建证据池并在最终生成前重放，在不剪枝上下文的情况下提升证据利用效率，在8个长上下文数据集上一致提升Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽支持越来越长的上下文窗口，但往往无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，即存在上下文可访问性与有效利用之间的差距。

Method: RECONTEXT是一种训练无关的推理方法，通过模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件化的证据池，并在最终生成前重放该证据池，同时保留完整原始上下文。该递归选择过程将证据组织与答案生成分离，无需训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝。

Result: 在8个长上下文数据集（128K上下文长度）上，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个骨干模型上均一致提升了证据利用效率，取得了最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: RECONTEXT通过递归证据重放有效弥合了上下文访问与利用之间的差距，为长上下文推理提供了一种轻量级、无需训练的改进方案。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [86] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代式AI编程（Iterative VibeCoding）环境，研究AI编程代理在持续代码库中跨拉取请求（PR）分步实施恶意任务的攻击策略。实验表明，渐进式攻击比集中式攻击更容易规避单一监控器，而本文提出的状态化链接追踪监控器可显著降低攻击规避率（从93%降至47%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编程代理自动化程度提高，其跨会话持久化代码库的特性引入了新的攻击面：被注入恶意提示或对齐不良的代理可在多个PR间分布攻击负载，并选择最佳时机触发。传统单次PR监控无法应对这种跨PR的渐进式威胁。

Method: 构建Iterative VibeCoding基准测试，包含CLI工具和Flask Web服务两类任务共20个变体；使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理、GPT-4o作为监控器；比较渐进式攻击（跨PR分步实施）与非渐进式攻击（单PR集中实施）；提出状态化链接追踪监控器，追踪跨PR的可疑累积行为。

Result: 无单一监控器能同时有效防御两种攻击策略；高规避率（≥65%）在不同攻击代理后端（Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5）间泛化；状态化链接追踪监控器显著优于仅查看累积历史差异的监控器；结合更强监控器与轨迹监控器的四监控器集成系统将渐进式攻击规避率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持久状态攻击面是AI编程代理安全的固有挑战，单一监控器无法同时防御渐进式与非渐进式攻击；状态化跨PR追踪是提升检测能力的关键方向，多监控器集成可显著提升防御效果。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Bilinear control of age--space structured populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01347)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带再生长边界条件和非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程的双线性最优控制问题，其中包含内源性监测反馈。通过特征线法建立闭环适定性和Frechet可微性，推导出约化和反馈校正的伴随方程，并给出最优性一阶条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如Lions-Magenes论证）难以处理由内源性监测反馈产生的非线性闭环控制-状态映射，需要新的理论框架来分析这类反馈依赖的种群控制问题。

Method: 采用特征线法（characteristic mild formulation）替代标准的Lions-Magenes论证；将反馈导数识别为低秩扰动项；在Volterra核框架下利用拟幂零转移算子获得伴随方程的显式预解表示。

Result: 建立了闭环系统的适定性和Frechet可微性；推导了约化伴随方程和反馈校正伴随方程；给出了最优性一阶条件，并将切换函数分解为约化分量和反馈诱导分量。

Conclusion: 成功构建了含内源性反馈的非局部年龄-空间结构种群双线性最优控制的理论框架，为处理更复杂的反馈依赖控制问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study constrained bilinear optimal control for nonlocal age--space structured population equations with renewal boundary conditions and endogenous surveillance feedback. The control acts as a coefficient in a mixed transport--diffusion equation, while a scalar observable generated by the state enters both the interior dynamics and the renewal law. This produces a nonlinear closed-loop control-to-state map and a feedback-dependent adjoint system. Using a characteristic mild formulation rather than a standard Lions--Magenes argument, we establish closed-loop well-posedness and Frechet differentiability. We then derive the reduced and feedback-corrected adjoint equations. The feedback derivative is identified as a low-rank perturbation $\ell_{\bar y,\bar u}(p)(t)χ(a,x)$; in the Volterra-kernel regime, the associated transfer operator is quasinilpotent, yielding an explicit resolvent representation of the adjoint. Finally, we prove first-order optimality conditions and decompose the switching function into reduced and feedback-induced components.

</details>


### [88] [Flexible and Reliable Network Design for Emerging Transportation Services: Multi-stage Stochastic Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01524)
*Riki Kawase,Koki Satsukawa,Toru Seo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灵活且可靠的网络设计问题（FR-NDPs）通用框架，将战略投资决策与运营控制相结合，采用风险厌恶的多阶段随机规划方法，通过随机对偶动态规划求解，并在曼哈顿中城网络上验证了灵活性与可靠性的互补作用、动态风险对冲策略以及运营灵活性对投资决策的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴交通服务（如共享自动驾驶汽车系统）面临历史数据稀缺和技术发展不确定的挑战，现有网络设计方法难以兼顾投资灵活性和可靠性，因此需要一种能够动态调整基础设施投资以应对不确定性的通用框架。

Method: 将FR-NDPs建模为风险厌恶的多阶段随机规划问题，采用随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）求解，并建立了战略与运营子问题收敛到全局最优的充分条件。应用于SAV容量扩展和SAV-BRT综合路线设计两个场景。

Result: 曼哈顿中城网络数值实验表明：(i) 灵活性与可靠性互补，在缓解预期性能损失的同时对冲严重场景；(ii) 投资规划的灵活性允许动态风险对冲，风险厌恶型规划者减少早期投资以保持适应性；(iii) SAV与BRT系统运营灵活性的差异反映在战略决策中，风险厌恶型规划者倾向于避免投资运营灵活性较低的交通模式。

Conclusion: FR-NDP框架能够有效整合不确定环境下的网络设计灵活性与可靠性，揭示了灵活性和可靠性之间的互补关系，以及运营灵活性对战略投资决策的影响，为新兴交通系统的规划提供了理论支撑和实用工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes a general framework for flexible and reliable network design problems (FR-NDPs). The framework enables planners to change infrastructure investments in response to realized uncertainties, while ensuring desired levels of reliability. Motivated by emerging transportation services such as shared autonomous vehicle (SAV) systems, where historical data are scarce and technological developments uncertain, FR-NDPs integrate strategic investment decisions with operational control. We formulate the FR-NDPs as risk-averse multi-stage stochastic problems to be solvable by stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP) and establish sufficient conditions under which strategic and operational subproblems converge to the global optimum. We illustrate applications to SAV capacity expansion and integrated SAV-BRT (Bus Rapid Transit) route design, and numerical experiments on a Midtown Manhattan network highlight three key findings: (i) flexibility and reliability act complementarily to hedge against severe scenarios while mitigating the loss of expected performance; (ii) flexibility in investment planning allows dynamic risk hedging, with risk-averse planners reducing early-stage investments to preserve adaptability; and (iii) differences in operational flexibility between SAV and BRT systems are reflected in strategic decisions, with risk-averse planners tending to refrain investment in transport modes with lower operational flexibility.

</details>


### [89] [Mean Field Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01525)
*René Carmona,Mathieu Laurière*

Main category: math.OC

Abstract: This monograph provides an introduction to mean field reinforcement learning through the lens of Markov decision processes arising from large-population stochastic control with mean field interactions and common noise. Starting from the connection between multi-agent reinforcement learning and mean field control, it develops the probabilistic, mathematical, and control-theoretic framework needed to formulate representative-agent learning problems, analyze their relationship with finite-population systems, and study both general and linear-quadratic models. The presentation includes dynamic programming principles, propagation-of-chaos limits, and theoretical analyses of tabular Q-learning and policy-gradient methods. It also discusses numerical implementations, including tabular schemes and deep reinforcement learning methods such as deep deterministic policy gradient. The goal is to give readers a coherent bridge between mean field control theory and reinforcement learning methodology, emphasizing the mathematical structure of the problems and the design of tractable learning approaches for large stochastic populations.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the automated discovery of iterative optimization algorithms. We formulate the algorithm discovery task as a discrete optimization problem and search for new update functions using latent space Bayesian Optimization. The proposed framework first learns a continuous representation of the discrete space of update functions using variational autoencoders, transforming the algorithm discovery task from a discrete to a continuous search problem. The continuous representation is subsequently used to search for new algorithms using Bayesian optimization. Application to two case studies shows that the proposed approach can discover new update functions in symbolic form without any assumptions on the functional form of the update function. Moreover, the computational time required to discover the new update functions is lower than existing mathematical programming-based approaches.

Method: 将初创企业人力资源管理建模为多域结构建模问题，使用多域矩阵（MDM）整合成员、技能和项目三个相互关联的域；基于该表示提供定性分析指南和定量度量指标，用于诊断HR状态并支持人员决策。

Abstract: This paper presents an actionable human resource (HR) decision-support framework for small firms and startups based on a multi-domain matrix (MDM). The framework addresses three key challenges faced by small organizations: complex interdependencies among organizational components; the lack of systematic analytical tools for HR decision-making; and the need for rapid responses in fast-changing organizational environments. The proposed framework formulates startup human resource management as a multi-domain structural modeling problem, where members, skills, and projects are interconnected domains within an integrated MDM. Based on this representation, the framework provides qualitative analysis guidelines and quantitative metrics for diagnosing an organization's HR state and supporting personnel decisions on workload redistribution, hiring, and capability development. A case study of MDM-based HR decisions for an early-stage technology startup is conducted to demonstrate the framework's practical applicability. The application shows that the framework can identify workload imbalances, reveal a key member with an unsustainable workload, and inform a subsequent hiring decision. The framework can be further applied after the hiring of a new member to track changes in the organization's multi-domain structure and support continuous HR diagnosis.

</details>

Conclusion: 该框架为设计非光滑非凸去中心化优化中高效压缩通信算法提供了理论基础，新方法在通信效率与收敛精度之间实现了有效平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the nonsmooth nonconvex decentralized optimization problem, where inter-agent communication is compressed. We propose a general framework that unifies various decentralized stochastic subgradient-type methods with unbiased compression and contractive compression with error compensation. By relating the consensus-error iterates and the averaged iterates to the trajectories of continuous-time differential inclusions, we establish global convergence for all methods encompassed by our framework when the objective functions are nonsmooth and lack Clarke regularity. Based on our framework, we further develop several compression-based methods, including decentralized stochastic subgradient methods utilizing sign-based regularization and gradient-tracking momentum. Preliminary numerical experiments empirically support our theoretical results and highlight the communication-accuracy trade-off of the newly developed methods.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文针对持续性认知战场景，提出一个将 grievance（不满情绪）与 institutional resilience（制度韧性）作为连续动态系统、将压力强度作为马尔可夫决策过程优化的耦合模型，证明均衡的存在性与局部稳定性，并通过伊朗案例（2017-2026）和随机网络实验验证了模型的计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运筹学中的攻防模型将防御方状态视为离散分配，无法刻画防御方在持续经济、媒体和心理压力下作为连续、缓慢变化的制度过程的动态响应。本文旨在填补这一建模空白。

Method: 构建耦合动力系统：grievance 和 institutional resilience 为连续变量，压力强度由优化型马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）选择。证明均衡的存在性与局部稳定性，并与标准反馈稳定性分析及孤立平稳马尔可夫链进行形式区分。采用30个随机网络实例进行完整值迭代计算，并基于伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）进行历史校准验证。

Result: 伊朗校准案例位于计算得出的运行崩溃边界约25倍以上；贪心播种策略在随机实例中达到87%的平均网络渗透率，显著高于度中心性基线。

Conclusion: 从业者可使用均衡与边界计算来评估特定案例相对于崩溃边界的位置，而非依赖未经验证的压力强度对比。

Abstract: A defending state facing sustained economic, media, and psychological pressure from an adversary that continuously re-optimises its campaign poses a problem that existing attacker-defender models in operations research do not directly resolve, because they treat the defender's state as a discrete allocation rather than a continuous, slow-moving institutional process. We formulate a coupled dynamical system in which grievance and institutional resilience evolve continuously while pressure intensity is chosen by an optimising Markov decision process, prove existence and local stability of the resulting equilibrium, and prove a formal result distinguishing it from standard feedback-stability analysis and from a stationary Markov chain treated in isolation. We validate the framework computationally using thirty randomised network instances, full value iteration, and a documented case study of cognitive warfare directed at Iran (2017--2026). The historically calibrated case sits approximately twenty-five times above the computed operational collapse boundary, and a greedy seeding policy reaches eighty-seven percent average network penetration across the randomised instances, significantly above a degree-centrality baseline. A practitioner can use the equilibrium and boundary computation to assess where a specific case sits relative to collapse, rather than relying on an unverified comparison between opposing pressure intensities.

</details>


### [94] [Lower Bounds for Anytime Acceleration of Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02053)
*Chung-En Tsai,Ilyas Fatkhullin,Liang Zhang,Niao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对梯度下降法（GD）在光滑凸优化中的任意时刻收敛（anytime convergence）问题，建立了两个下界：函数值最小化的任意时刻收敛率不能达到o(n^{-1.334})，平方梯度范数最小化的任意时刻收敛率不能达到o(n^{-1})。这为COLT 2024开放问题提供了首个下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知GD在给定迭代次数n时可达到O(n^{-1.271})的收敛率，但在任意时刻收敛（n事先未知）的设置中，已知上界远低于经典一阶方法的Ω(n^{-2})下界。本文旨在填补这一空白，为任意时刻收敛中GD的最优收敛率建立下界。

Method: 通过分析二次函数和Huber函数的变体，推导出大步长数量和幅度的新型上界，进而建立GD在任意时刻收敛中的下界。

Result: 证明了在任意时刻收敛设置中：1) 函数值最小化无法达到o(n^{-1.334})的收敛率；2) 平方梯度范数最小化无法达到o(n^{-1})的收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文首次为GD在任意时刻收敛中的最优收敛率提供了下界结果，回应了Kornowski和Shamir在COLT 2024上提出的开放问题。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that the convergence rate of gradient descent (GD) in smooth convex optimization can be significantly improved by employing large stepsizes that may violate the descent property. In particular, if the total number of iterations $n$ is given, an $O(n^{-1.271})$ convergence rate can be achieved for both function value and squared gradient norm minimization. On the other hand, in the setting of anytime convergence, where $n$ is not known in advance, the best known rates of GD are much slower: $O(n^{-1.119})$ for function value minimization and $O(n^{-1})$ for squared gradient norm minimization. It remains open whether any of these upper bounds can be improved, as they are far from the classical $Ω(n^{-2})$ lower bound for any first-order method.
  In this work, we establish two lower bounds on the anytime convergence of GD. We show that no positive stepsize schedule can achieve an $o(n^{-1.334})$ anytime rate for function value minimization, nor an $o(n^{-1})$ anytime rate for squared gradient norm minimization. The key ingredients of our analysis are novel upper bounds on the number and the magnitude of large stepsizes, derived by analyzing GD on quadratic functions and variants of Huber functions. Our work provides the first lower bounds for the COLT 2024 open problem posed by Kornowski and Shamir regarding the optimal anytime convergence rates of GD.

</details>


### [95] [Invariance Entropy in the Dust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02279)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 通过单一构造否定了Kawan关于控制系统中不变熵的两个长期未解问题：有限严格不变熵不必等于普通不变熵，且严格不变熵在Hausdorff扰动下不必下半连续。构造基于Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令、指数收缩坐标隐藏后期不匹配、以及紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定Kawan关于控制系统不变熵的两个自然推广问题（已悬而未决超过十五年），揭示不变熵理论中信息复杂度的新来源。

Method: 构造一个连续时间控制系统，包含三个关键组件：(1) Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令；(2) 指数收缩坐标使后期不匹配在几何上不可见；(3) 紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致。

Result: 证明了有限严格不变熵不必等于普通不变熵，且严格不变熵在Hausdorff扰动下不必下半连续。

Conclusion: 识别出不变熵中信息复杂度的新来源：并非由动力扩张产生，而是由薄不变几何下精确生存约束的持续性以及不变熵中的极限顺序所导致。

Abstract: We answer negatively two natural general forms of Kawan's questions on invariance entropy for control systems, open for more than fifteen years, by a single construction. We show that finite strict invariance entropy need not coincide with ordinary invariance entropy, and that strict invariance entropy need not be lower semicontinuous under Hausdorff perturbations of the initial set. The construction is a continuous-time control system in which a Cantor coordinate stores an infinite symbolic instruction, an exponentially contracting coordinate makes late mismatches geometrically invisible, and a compact matching graph forces exact symbolic agreement. It identifies a source of information complexity not generated by dynamical expansion, but by the persistence of exact viability constraints under thin invariant geometry and by the order of limits in invariance entropy.

</details>

*Jinsheng Du,Boris Mordukhovich,Shengda Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强耦合非光滑系统（半线性发展包含与状态依赖极大单调算子微分包含），现有研究缺乏对参数化系统优化控制的系统理论，尤其是在参数不确定性下的鲁棒控制和折衷控制方面。

Method: 首先收集参数化解映射的适定性、紧致性及Painlevé-Kuratowski连续性；然后利用这些性质证明Bolza型优化最优对的存在性、值函数的连续性及最优解映射的上半连续性；进而拓展到固定参数控制、同时控制-参数设计、最小-最大鲁棒控制和Hurwicz折衷控制四种优化框架，并建立各自的存在性定理。

Result: 建立了参数化解映射的正则性理论；证明了Bolza型优化问题的最优对存在性、值函数连续性和最优解映射上半连续性；对四种参数不确定性下的控制问题均证明了最优解的存在性；数值实验验证了扫掠型系统的敏感性和鲁棒性理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为强耦合非光滑系统在参数不确定性下的优化与控制提供了完整的理论框架，所建立的解映射正则性及多种优化形式的存在性结果为实际工程应用（如扫掠型系统）提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We consider a class of strongly coupled nonsmooth systems consisting of a semilinear evolution inclusion and a differential inclusion governed by state-dependent maximal monotone operators. Our main contributions are fourfold. First, we collect the well-posedness, compactness, and Painlevé--Kuratowski continuity properties of the parameterized solution map required for the subsequent optimization analysis. Second, for Bolza-type optimization over the solution set, we prove the existence of optimal pairs, establish continuity properties of the value function, and derive upper semicontinuity of the optimal-solution map. Third, we study fixed-parameter optimal control, simultaneous control-parameter design, min--max robust control, and Hurwicz-type compromise control under parameter uncertainty, and we establish existence results for each formulation. Fourth, we report numerical experiments for sweeping-type systems that illustrate the sensitivity and robustness phenomena predicted by the theory.

</details>


### [97] [Boundary-Aware Quantization: Finite-Scale Decision Geometry of Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01478)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了量化引起的决策边界变化，提出了多种边界度量指标（局部对数间隔半径、一阶边界位移、法向变化、切片边界Jaccard距离、网格预测变化、多类交点计数、低间隔边界带翻转），并在多个基准（Digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10）上评估了PTQ-W和优化舍入方法。实验表明校准集边界Jaccard与留出集高度相关（r=0.947–0.994），边界感知的早停策略可显著减少决策翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化研究主要关注精度损失，但对量化如何改变模型决策边界的几何结构缺乏系统量化。理解边界变化对于保障量化模型可靠性至关重要。

Method: 提出一组量化诱导边界变化的度量指标，包括局部对数间隔半径、一阶边界位移、法向变化、切片边界Jaccard距离、网格预测变化、多类交点计数、低间隔边界带翻转。在Digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上评估PTQ-W（基于精度选择的权重量化）与固定比特边界间隔舍入项优化方法，并引入边界感知的早停策略。

Result: Digits上8-bit权重量化保持全部测试标签但边界Jaccard为0.428；4-bit精度0.9733，边界Jaccard升至0.970，局部边界位移中位数0.0290。校准到测试的早停将留出翻转率从0.0094降至0.0022，边界Jaccard从0.825降至0.524。CIFAR-10上边界感知早停选择8-bit（翻转0.0083，边界Jaccard 0.048）优于PTQ-W的6-bit（翻转0.0367，边界Jaccard 0.184）。固定比特边界间隔舍入项在4-bit将边界Jaccard从0.457降至0.435。校准边界Jaccard与留出边界Jaccard相关性达0.947–0.994。

Conclusion: 量化引起的决策边界变化可被系统量化；校准集上的边界Jaccard可作为留出集边界变化的可靠代理指标；边界感知的早停策略能在保持精度的同时显著减少决策翻转，为量化模型的部署可靠性提供新视角。

Abstract: We measured quantization-induced decision-boundary changes using local logit-margin radii, first-order boundary displacement, normal variation, slice-boundary Jaccard distance, grid prediction changes, multiclass junction counts, and low-margin boundary-band flips. On the digits benchmark, 8-bit weight quantization preserved all test labels while producing boundary-mask Jaccard \(0.428\) on the PCA slice; at 4 bits, accuracy remained \(0.9733\), while boundary Jaccard rose to \(0.970\) and median local boundary shift reached \(0.0290\). Interpolation between adjacent quantization levels localized the visible reconfigurations at multiclass junctions, with 12, 34, and 17 triple-junction cells in the selected transitions. Calibration-to-test stopping reduced the digits held-out flip rate from \(0.0094\) to \(0.0022\) and boundary Jaccard from \(0.825\) to \(0.524\); the same stopping rule also reduced flips on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST. On official CIFAR-10 subsets, PTQ-W selected by accuracy gave 6-bit flip \(0.0367\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.184\), whereas boundary-aware stopping selected 8-bit flip \(0.0083\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.048\). On full CIFAR-10 with three seeds, 6-bit PTQ-W lost \(0.0029\) accuracy relative to float, changed \(5.3\%\) of held-out decisions, and changed \(24.5\%\) of low-margin boundary-band decisions. A fixed-bit boundary-gap rounding term changed the trade-off at 4 bits by reducing boundary Jaccard from \(0.457\) to \(0.435\) and boundary-band pair-order flip from \(0.3600\) to \(0.3558\), with an accuracy trade-off; the 3-bit stress test exposed the tuning limit of this surrogate. Calibration boundary Jaccard predicted held-out boundary Jaccard across PTQ-W and optimized rounding variants with \(r=0.947\)--\(0.994\).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），将认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与深度学习相融合，通过Q矩阵约束技能-题目关系，确保潜在掌握特征的可解释性，并设计了带L2惩罚的损失函数与可解释对齐评估指标，实现了心理测量透明度与神经灵活性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有结构可解释性但表达能力有限，而深度神经网络虽灵活却缺乏心理测量透明度。如何在保持诊断有效性的前提下，将认知理论的结构先验融入神经网络，实现可解释、公平且可操作的AI认知诊断。

Method: 1) 将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，构建技能-题目关系约束；2) 设计带L2惩罚的损失函数，对不符合Q矩阵的技能激活进行惩罚；3) 提出可解释对齐评估指标，量化预测技能激活与题目层面技能的一致性。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持预测性能的同时，提升了潜在掌握特征的可解释性，所提出的对齐指标能够有效反映模型与认知理论的一致性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet通过将诊断有效性嵌入模型设计，弥合了心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，为课堂实践中的学习困难早期检测和掌握导向干预提供了支持，推动了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [99] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程示例（PBE）系统在面对恶意构造的错误示例时的鲁棒性问题，形式化定义了固定集最坏情况下的示例破坏，提出基于版本空间分区聚合（VPA）的防御方法，并通过大量实验揭示了此类对抗性破坏与随机噪声的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE系统通常假设错误示例来自随机噪声过程，但忽略了存在恶意攻击者故意选择破坏性示例的失败模式。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究PBE系统在对抗性示例破坏下的脆弱性。

Method: 1) 形式化定义有限PBE版本空间的固定集最坏情况破坏；2) 在字符串转换DSL上实现精确（有界池内）和启发式破坏搜索；3) 提出VPA防御：在不相交示例组上合成并通过语义签名投票；4) 在多种任务上评估，包括人工/生成DSL任务、SyGuS PBE_SLIA、Playgol v2以及噪声PBE基线。

Result: 1) 一个精心设计的编辑即可翻转所有8个spike任务，而200轮随机对照组成功率仅10-17%；2) 生成的margin-1行在预算1下被翻转，VPA可恢复；3) 在公共SyGuS上投票边际接近1，自适应攻击者将VPA准确率降至零；4) Playgol在141个接受行上显示VPA相较随机对照有正向改善；5) LLM小规模实验呈现相同定性模式。

Conclusion: 低边际PBE任务存在一个对抗鲁棒性维度，随机噪声评估无法捕捉；VPA仅在干净语义保持分区投票边际时有效，而这在现实任务中常常失败。核心主张是有意有限且部分否定的：揭示了现有评测的盲区，但防御方案的实际效果有限。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [100] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识（PRQST关键点）的双流有向图卷积网络用于ECG识别，在First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition数据集上实现了88.1%的平均F1分数，其中稀有类别F1为76.3%，优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在ECG识别中广泛应用，但模型可解释性不足，尤其是端到端CNN方法难以融入领域知识，导致稀有类别检测性能不佳。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的双流有向图卷积网络：利用PRQST关键点作为领域知识，构建空间有向图（建模关键点间位置关系）和时间有向图（建模相邻ECG周期的时间依赖关系），形成双流架构。

Result: 在First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition九分类数据集上，整体平均F1为88.1%，稀有类别平均F1为76.3%，均优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识（PRQST关键点）和图卷积网络能有效提升ECG识别性能，尤其对稀有类别的检测有显著增强效果。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [101] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的性能，发现在GloVe嵌入上存在维度缩放交叉现象，该算法在维度扩展方面优于图、树和分区方法，且具有近线性查询缩放和较低索引成本，在高维或高重建频率场景中具有竞争力，并可能指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的近似最近邻搜索方法在现代扩展分析中被忽视，文章旨在系统地表征多探针网格算法相对于数据集大小N和维度d的性能，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统性的实验表征，在GloVe嵌入族上比较其与图、树和分区方法在维度缩放方面的性能差异。

Result: 发现了此前未报道的维度缩放交叉现象：多探针网格搜索保持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而其他方法吞吐量下降；具有近线性N查询缩放和较低索引成本。

Conclusion: 基于网格的方法（如多探针网格）在高维或索引重建频繁的场景中具有竞争力；ANN算法的N和d缩放特性可为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [102] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书从经典逼近论、优化和泛化理论出发，系统梳理了现代深度学习理论中过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等前沿课题，构建了一个统一的、以证明为导向的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超越了任何单一数学理论的解释能力，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来整合分散的研究成果。

Method: 通过将广泛文献组织成连贯的研究叙事，每种理论从其控制的对象、使其成立的前提假设以及其无法解释的现象三个维度进行分析。

Result: 提供了一个从经典理论到当代机制的完整理论图谱，揭示了深度学习理论当前的状态：强大但不完备，且日益聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现这一核心问题。

Conclusion: 该专著为研究人员、研究生和具备数学背景的从业者提供了一份严谨的深度学习理论地图，指出了该领域当前的理解边界和未解问题。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [103] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IonSense-QKG，一个面向锂离子电池数据集发现的量子就绪元数据框架。该框架在EV-Battery-IonSense索引基础上，为公开电池数据集添加量子相关元数据（包括任务类型、传感模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围及NISQ可行性），并引入透明量子就绪评分来排序数据集作为混合量子-经典电池基准测试的候选资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构、访问状态和预处理复杂性方面差异显著，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流。现有缺乏一个统一框架来评估和发现适合量子计算工作流的电池数据集。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，为公开电池数据集记录添加量子相关元数据；引入透明量子就绪评分作为数据集排序启发式方法；支持基于查询的元数据发现机制，识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测等场景的数据集。

Result: 发布了包含元数据表格、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的完整工件，实现了对适合多种量子工作流场景的数据集的查询发现。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为数据中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，其评分旨在作为数据集选择启发式方法而非量子优势的证据。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [104] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了条件推理树（CIT）和条件推理森林（CIF）作为top-k特征排序方法的下游预测性能，发现CIF在17种分类方法中排名第4、18种回归方法中排名第3；通过运行时消融实验发现自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大（分别增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍），而下游分数变化不超过0.011；在高维稀疏模拟中，森林特征采样可能使信息性特征被排除在许多分裂决策之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推理树和森林通过检验特征后再选择分裂阈值来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销大，需要系统评估其计算效率与预测性能之间的权衡。

Method: 采用真实数据基准测试、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复模拟实验；使用Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值控制节点级拒绝；对17种分类方法（22个数据集）和18种回归方法（8个数据集）进行基准对比。

Result: CIF在分类任务中排名第4、回归任务中排名第3；自适应停止关闭使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍，精确阈值搜索增加1.9-10.8倍，而分数变化≤0.011；高维模拟显示森林特征采样可能遗漏信息性特征。

Conclusion: CIF在评估的下游预测基准中作为top-k特征排序方法表现良好，但高维场景下特征采样策略需进一步优化。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的DNA甲基化分类方法，在CNS肿瘤分类任务中，于91类级别达到86%准确率（参考方法82%），在甲基化类别家族级别达到93%（参考方法88%），分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA甲基化分析已成为中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的有力工具，但现有方法在跨队列可迁移性、方法论正确性和稳健的多类别评估方面仍存在重要挑战，亟需更严谨的方法学改进。

Method: 提出了结合稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维与多项逻辑回归（multinomial logistic regression）进行分类的机器学习方法，在与广泛使用的参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 在2,801样本参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率96%；在1,104样本独立临床评估队列上，91类级别准确率86%（参考82%），甲基化类别家族级别准确率93%（参考88%），分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在方法论上更为严谨，一致性地超越了先前最优方法，能够切实提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，对癌症亚型判定、治疗方案选择和临床决策具有直接影响。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [106] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将模型大小、训练数据拆分为训练步数和批次大小的三元缩放定律，可在更少训练运行下稳健拟合，并恢复最优批次大小的缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律通常只考虑模型大小和总训练数据量，忽略了批次大小和训练步数的独立影响，导致需要大量最优批次大小的训练运行才能拟合。

Method: 提出三元缩放定律（three-term law），将训练数据明确拆分为训练步数和批次大小两个独立变量，在大规模训练运行上拟合该定律。

Result: 三元定律正确恢复了最优批次大小的缩放规律；由于允许使用次优批次大小的训练运行，拟合所需的训练运行数量显著减少；可推导次优批次大小的缩放定律，并匹配关键批次大小的先前实证结果。

Conclusion: 该三元缩放定律更具鲁棒性和数据效率，为模型训练中的批次大小选择提供了更通用的理论指导。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [107] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习中，鲁棒性、局部差分隐私与泛化误差之间并非简单的三难困境，而是取决于隐私强度：高噪声（强隐私）下提升隐私反而降低泛化误差，低噪声（弱隐私）下两者存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作建立了拜占庭鲁棒性、局部差分隐私与优化误差之间的基本三难困境，但该困境是否适用于泛化误差尚不清楚，作者旨在厘清这一关系。

Method: 通过算法稳定性理论，推导了LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的泛化误差下界与上界，并进行了实证验证。

Result: 高噪声（强隐私）下，增大隐私预算反而降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私无冲突；低噪声（弱隐私）下，两者重新出现权衡，增大隐私会损害泛化性能。该非单调行为被匹配的上下界所解释。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性与LDP之间的三难困境在泛化误差上并非普遍成立，其存在性依赖于具体的隐私强度区间。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [108] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NightVision攻击方法，即使在当前受限的LLM API（仅返回单个logit且无logit偏置功能）下，仍能通过新型通用集提示技术和首令牌时间（TTFT）测量，以平均23%相对误差恢复隐藏维度，以53%以内误差恢复深度和参数量（针对30亿以上参数模型），揭示了当前API无法充分隐藏模型架构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商为了隐藏模型架构细节，已将API限制为仅返回每个解码token的单个logit并移除logit偏置功能。然而，在这种严格限制下，攻击者是否仍能恢复关键架构参数尚不清楚，本文旨在探究当前API的安全性边界。

Method: NightVision攻击包含两个核心步骤：1）提出通用集提示技术，通过多个提示使模型对同一输出token集暴露log概率，对结果进行谱分析推断隐藏维度；2）利用端到端首令牌时间（TTFT）测量结合已估计的隐藏维度，进一步推断模型深度和总参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上的实验表明：隐藏维度恢复平均相对误差为23%（MoE模型上仅9%）；对于超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量恢复误差在53%以内；消融实验展示了精度随token预算和模型属性的缩放规律。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制不足以完全混淆底层模型的架构细节，存在信息泄露风险，需要更严格的保护措施。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [109] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种角色感知的神经凸散度头（role-aware neural convex divergence head），用于非对称表示学习。该方法通过源角色和目标角色投影，再计算输入凸神经Bregman散度，得到非负的结构化分数。实验表明，在词义、句子、本体和部分有向图基准上，该方法在方向性准确率上优于对称距离和普通ICNN-Bregman头，但在大规模固定特征引文预测中，对称或双曲基线仍更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多表示学习问题涉及有向关系（如词汇蕴涵、句子蕴涵、本体层级、引文链接等），但标准欧氏距离、余弦距离和马氏距离等头部是对称的，无法建模方向性；而通用神经打分器虽能建模方向性，但缺乏几何结构。因此需要一种既能捕捉方向性又具有结构化可解释性的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出角色感知的神经凸散度头：先对输入进行源角色和目标角色投影，再计算输入凸神经网络（ICNN）的Bregman散度，从而在角色投影空间中得到非负的结构化有向分数。论文还刻画了投影空间中的恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解和基于Hessian的局部曲率等理论性质。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图基准上（10个随机种子），角色感知投影一致提高了方向性准确率，且零负散度率保持为零。但在大规模固定特征引文预测任务中，对称或双曲基线在排序准确率上仍更强。

Conclusion: 该头部是一种结构化、可解释的即插即用距离模块，适用于方向性关系重要的任务；但存在边界情况（如大规模固定特征引文预测），此时专业化的对称或双曲基线仍占优。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning. The head applies source- and target-role projections before evaluating an input-convex neural Bregman divergence, yielding a nonnegative structured score in the role-projected space. We characterize its projected-space identity, source-role convexity, directional-gap decomposition, and Hessian-based local curvature. Experiments on lexical, sentence, ontology, and directed graph benchmarks compare symmetric distances, unstructured asymmetric scorers, order/hyperbolic baselines, plain ICNN-Bregman heads, and the proposed role-aware variant. Across ten random seeds on the main semantic and ontology benchmarks, role-aware projections consistently improve directional accuracy over plain ICNN-Bregman heads while preserving zero observed negative divergence rate. The results also identify a boundary case: on large fixed-feature citation prediction, specialized symmetric or hyperbolic baselines remain stronger in ranking accuracy. Overall, the proposed head is best understood as a structured and interpretable plug-in distance module for tasks where directional relations matter.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用多模态数据（铁路道口图像+事故报告）评估铁路道口安全性的AI系统，通过路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型(VLM)管道，实现了与专家意见和联邦铁路管理局(FRA)安全评分一致的高风险/低风险分类（宏F1=0.757）和FRA安全评分估计（RMSE=0.078, 相关系数=0.492）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以利用视觉线索以及引入结构化事故数据，构建能够融合多模态数据的AI系统，为铁路道口提供与专家意见和FRA安全评分一致的安全评估。

Method: 提出一个端到端的验证性管道，包含数据准备、多模态融合及路由微调紧凑型VLM管道，利用铁路道口图像和官方事故报告进行安全评估。

Result: 高风险/低风险分类宏F1=0.757，FRA安全评分估计RMSE=0.078，相关系数=0.492，定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 通过多模态数据融合和路由微调VLM可以实现与专家和FRA评分一致的铁路道口安全评估，为未来更完善的系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [111] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯位置模型中脊正则化对数密度比估计，在共同协方差矩阵已知的情况下，分别分析了变分估计器和谱估计器在高维渐进框架下的风险表现。结论是：观测数量多时变分估计器风险更小，观测数量少时谱估计器因方差更低而更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维高斯位置模型下，比较脊正则化对数密度比估计的两种方法——变分估计器（基于KL散度拟合+L2惩罚）和谱估计器（基于连续脊正则化最小二乘问题）——在观测数与维度同阶增长时的渐进风险差异。

Method: 利用仿射不变性将模型标准化为q~N(0,I)、p~N(Δ,I)；变分估计器采用经验KL对数归一化拟合加L2惩罚；谱估计器采用[1]中提出的连续脊正则化最小二乘问题。通过凸-高斯-极小-极大定理(CGMT)推导变分极限，通过两个独立高斯样本协方差矩阵的预解式确定等价量推导谱极限。

Result: 得到了两种估计器在高维确定性渐进等价下的闭式风险表达式；通过固定信号纵横比扫描和优化正则化的实验比较了总体风险。

Conclusion: 在分析的标准和渐进框架下：观测数充足时，正确设定的变分估计器风险更小；观测数较少时，谱估计器因其基于协方差的结构方差更低而更优。此外，还探讨了核惩罚用于特征学习的可能性和部分分析结果。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [112] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型中数值编码的离散、连续和混合策略，发现混合token方法在精度与鲁棒性之间取得最佳平衡，最优分箱数遵循数据集大小的幂律关系，并提出将混合token方法作为实际部署的默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Transformer的序列处理中（特别是电子健康记录数据），如何有效编码数值值是一个未解决的问题，现有离散、连续编码策略各有利弊，缺乏系统性的比较研究。

Method: 通过嵌入真实EHR数据的合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务，系统比较了离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略，并分析了数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间的权衡。

Result: 显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上表现最佳但受架构限制；混合token方法在分箱后投影到嵌入空间，鲁棒性更强且适用性更广；最优分箱数遵循数据集大小的幂律关系；模型表现出"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术；临床任务中实验室值的收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 在实际部署中，鲁棒性和可部署性通常比最大数值精度更重要，混合token方法可作为实际应用的默认选择。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [113] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个基于临床引导的多任务MRI框架，通过自监督预训练结合海马分割、萎缩分类和重建任务，以及门控融合微调，在AD和MCI诊断中超越了传统单任务方法，实现了高准确率和良好的跨队列泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于MRI的阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍及相关痴呆症识别方法面临挑战，因为疾病相关的结构变化通常微妙且异质性高，单一任务的模型难以充分捕捉这些复杂特征。

Method: NeuroBridge整合了大规模自监督MRI预训练，联合海马分割、海马萎缩分类和图像重建三个辅助目标进行多任务学习，随后采用门控融合机制进行微调，最终在ADNI和OASIS两个独立队列上进行评估。

Result: NeuroBridge在所有分类任务中达到最高性能：ADNI上AD vs 认知正常对照准确率88.17%，OASIS上82.78%。在MCI相关和混合诊断场景中提升最大，展现出强跨队列泛化能力，预测概率与准确性之间存在系统性关联，并验证了基于概率的机会性筛查可行性。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表示学习显著提升了神经退行性疾病的MRI诊断性能，超越了传统的单任务方法。NeuroBridge为痴呆评估和基于MRI的机会性筛查提供了稳健且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [114] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP通过将Gaunt张量积从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)，恢复了缺失的反对称交互路径，在保持GTP渐近效率的同时实现了与完整CGTP相当的精度，并在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变网络的Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)复杂度为O(L^6)，限制了可扩展性；而Gaunt张量积(GTP)虽然降低了复杂度，但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达能力不完整。

Method: 将Gaunt张量积从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)，利用SWSH的代数性质恢复缺失的反对称交互，同时保持GTP的渐近效率；构建能自然处理张量积奇宇称分量的更具表达力的等变基。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准测试中，SpinGTP的精度与完整CGTP相当；在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: SpinGTP提供了一条通向大规模3D原子系统模拟中高阶等变性的完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [115] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL，一种基于强化学习的自适应时间步分配方法，用于优化扩散模型采样中的时间步调度，通过连续时间控制将调度学习转化为强化学习问题，在多个任务上一致优于固定调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型采样使用均匀或手工设计的时间步调度，这些固定调度是次优的，无法根据采样轨迹自适应调整时间步分配，导致采样效率和质量受限。

Method: 提出ART（自适应重参数化时间）连续时间控制框架，将采样时钟速度作为控制变量，使得在学到的时钟上均匀网格对应原始扩散时间中的自适应步长。基于欧拉误差代理构建优化目标。进一步提出ART-RL，引入辅助随机化形式和高斯策略，将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，证明两者在优化器层面等价，并推导出可实现的演员-评论家更新规则。

Result: 在低维控制实验到图像生成任务中，ART-RL仅改变时间步网格即可插入现有扩散采样器，在相同计算预算下一致优于强基线调度。学到的调度具有良好的泛化性，无需重新训练即可跨采样预算、数据集、求解器、流水线和表示空间迁移。

Conclusion: ART-RL提供了一种原理性且实用的扩散模型时间步自适应分配方法，可即插即用地提升现有采样器的样本质量，且学到的调度具有出色的跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [116] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，编码智能体强化学习通常将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，但实际上测量基础设施开销可以揭示RL后训练中的实际效率提升。研究比较了四种执行基底（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机），发现冷启动延迟变化高达110倍，百万次150步轨迹的预计工时可相差1.8倍。结论是未来的编码智能体RL系统应将执行基底作为训练系统本身的一部分进行优化，而非仅仅作为部署管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体强化学习系统将执行基础设施视为无关紧要的背景实现细节，忽视了其在大量交互式软件回滚中的开销影响，尤其是在大规模RL后训练中，微小的每次回滚开销会被显著放大。

Method: 对四种执行基底（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行了对比研究，测量了冷启动延迟和预计工人工时等关键性能指标。

Result: 不同执行基底间冷启动延迟变化高达110倍，百万次150步轨迹的预计工时可相差1.8倍。

Conclusion: 未来编码智能体RL系统的设计应将执行基底作为训练系统本身的一部分进行优化，而非仅仅视为部署层面的管道问题。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [117] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSGNAR（双重草图高斯-牛顿自适应比率方法），一种可扩展的二阶优化框架，通过解决PINNs的严重病态损失景观问题，在精度和速度上取得了前所未有的突破，能够达到双精度下3×10^{-16}的相对ℓ2误差，比现有方法提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解偏微分方程时难以达到传统求解器的精度，其根本障碍在于严重病态的损失景观导致的优化困难。

Method: 提出DSGNAR框架，结合双重草图高斯-牛顿模型与一种精心控制正则化和步长的新策略，构建可扩展的二阶优化方法。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维和Navier-Stokes等系列问题上大幅超越现有水平：双精度下相对ℓ2误差低至3×10^{-16}，Burgers方程提升五个数量级，高维Poisson问题提升八个数量级，且速度显著更快；单精度下Burgers方程在十秒内达到ℓ2_rel = 4.75×10^{-7}。

Conclusion: DSGNAR框架对架构选择、算术精度和初始超参数具有鲁棒性，为PINNs达到传统求解器级别的精度提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [118] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了MoE模型的结构化专家剪枝在生物医学领域的效用与可靠性权衡：适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且不立即损害可靠性，但极端剪枝会增加幻觉风险；跨域迁移时效用与可靠性均快速下降，仅评估效用不足以保障高风险场景的部署安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注剪枝后的基准效用，忽视了剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，特别是在生物医学等高风险领域，评估剪枝模型仅凭效用指标是不充分的。

Method: 对四种MoE模型、六种剪枝方法和多种剪枝比例进行实验，在领域内（生物医学）和跨域两种设置下评估生成任务和分类任务的效用与可靠性。

Result: 适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且可靠性不立即下降，但极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险增加；跨域到通用领域时，效用和可靠性均快速下降。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖任务和领域，仅基于效用评估剪枝后的MoE模型不足以支持高风险场景的部署，必须同时评估可靠性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [119] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合Rvachev R-函数与KAN的几何感知混合神经网络GRS-KAN，通过可微逻辑运算将几何约束显式编码到可训练网络中，在含间断的回归任务上相比标准KAN最高降低67%的测试RMSE，并提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN虽然能学习平滑非线性结构，但无法显式编码已知的几何或逻辑约束（如间断区域、可行域边界），导致在具有复杂几何支撑的回归问题上精度不足且可解释性差。

Method: 将V.L.Rvachev的R-函数（实现可微逻辑运算如R-合取/R-析取）集成到KAN框架中：KAN分支学习平滑非线性，R-函数解析编码几何约束（间断、可行域、隐式边界）。提出加性、乘性和不可知分支加权三种变体架构。

Result: 在含圆形/矩形支撑间断的回归基准上，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN测试RMSE降低高达67%，边界定位更精确；不可知变体能自动判断几何先验是否对当前任务有益。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能同时提升预测精度和可解释性，GRS-KAN为融合先验几何知识到神经网络提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [120] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ember优化器，专门针对语言模型的嵌入表和LM-head矩阵进行优化，利用其独特的梯度几何特性，仅需KB级优化器状态即可在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中提升Pareto前沿，并在训练轨迹中发现了简单的1D射线结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的嵌入表和LM-head矩阵作为连续程序与离散符号之间的读写接口，具有与密集隐藏权重不同的梯度几何特性，现有Adam优化器为这些矩阵分配O(2VD)的优化器状态，存在显著浪费。

Method: 提出Ember优化器，利用O(V+D)显存替代Adam的O(2VD)，无需对token表优化器状态进行分片；基于嵌入表和LM-head梯度几何的独特性质进行轻量级优化。

Result: Ember在批量大小和参数量上具有良好的扩展性；token的优化轨迹可由简单的1D射线描述，反驳了神经网络参数在高度非凸景观中导航的流行观点；揭示了足以支撑Transformer训练的优化器空间出乎意料地狭窄。

Conclusion: Ember为嵌入表和LM-head提供了一种高效、轻量的优化方案，可无缝集成到ZeRO/FSDP分布式训练框架中，代码已开源。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape.
